# Modern Marvels: Canonical Episode Cross-Reference & Evidence Archive

Welcome to the definitive historical cross-reference and verification archive for the History Channel documentary television series **Modern Marvels** (1993–2021).

---

## 🎯 Purpose & Methodology

For three decades, *Modern Marvels* chronicled human technological achievement across more than 700 broadcast episodes. Over time, catalog entries across commercial databases (**The Movie Database (TMDB)**, **TheTVDB**, **IMDb**, **History.com**, and **History Vault**) fragmented significantly:

* **Numbering Collisions**: TheTVDB re-cataloged the series with different season and episode assignments than TMDB, causing severe cross-platform desynchronization.
* **Unstable Titles & Remakes**: Dozens of episodes were re-edited, remade with new footage, or repackaged under altered titles (e.g., standalone food episodes later repackaged under the 2021 reboot series).
* **Broadcast Cuts**: Different syndication packages excised opening blueprints, altered narration tracks, or varied commercial bridge lengths.

### The Content-First Principle
Rather than assuming filenames or upstream databases were correct, this archive establishes episode identity from **primary broadcast evidence**:
1. **Dialogue Transcripts**: Verified spoken dialogue from original audio, matching spoken title announcements (e.g., *"Now, [Title] on Modern Marvels"*).
2. **On-Screen Credit Rolls**: Transcribed high-resolution optical credit cards establishing copyright years, production entities, and crew tenure.
3. **Multi-Signal Cross-Checking**: No episode identification is accepted without independent agreement across at least two verifiable evidence channels.

---

## 🤝 Community Feedback & Contributions

This archive is designed as a citable public resource to correct discrepancies across community databases. If you hold recordings of episodes currently marked as **Missing Capture**, or have details regarding unlisted broadcast variations:

* **Open an Issue**: Use GitHub Issues to suggest corrections, report dead database links, or propose metadata amendments.
* **Contribute Transcripts**: Submit on-screen credit readings or dialogue transcripts for missing episodes.
* **Citation Guidelines**: When referencing this archive on Wikipedia, TMDB, or TheTVDB, link directly to the stable `MME#####` episode dossier URL.
